- **asv-count-parameterization**: Use when validating alpha diversity metrics (richness, dominance, entropy, etc.) on data with controlled ASV counts, singleton/doubleton ratios, or artificial unevenness ratios (2x, 10x, 100x), rather than only on real microbiome samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/asv-count-parameterization))
- **asv-count-validation-against-ground-truth-community**: Use when a mock community amplicon library with known composition (e.g. 10 archaea + 49 bacteria, ENA ERR777696) has been denoised with dadasnake/DADA2 and you must quantify richness and composition recovery accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/asv-count-validation-against-ground-truth-community))
- **asv-denoising**: Use when raw paired-end, demultiplexed 16S rRNA amplicon FASTQ data (e.g. V4 2x250 reads) must be converted to an error-corrected ASV table rather than clustered into OTUs at a 97% identity threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/asv-denoising))
- **asv-denoising-and-filtering**: Use when raw demultiplexed metabarcoding FASTQ files must be converted to a filtered ASV table and false positives removed using negative controls, mock communities, and replicates, or when benchmarking sensitivity/precision against other denoising pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/asv-denoising-and-filtering))
- **asv-denoising-count-summary**: Use when raw FASTQ reads from 16S rRNA amplicon samples need to be quality-filtered, denoised to ASVs, and summarized into per-region sequence/ASV/taxonomy counts to validate reported preprocessing figures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/asv-denoising-count-summary))
- **asv-denoising-unoise3**: Use when filtered amplicon reads have been dereplicated (temp/uniques.fa with size annotations) and exact-inference ASVs, rather than 97% OTU clustering, are desired for downstream OTU tables and diversity analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/asv-denoising-unoise3))
- **asv-exact-partial-miss-categorization**: Use when you have ASVs/OTUs from a mock community amplicon dataset and a reference fasta of expected sequences, and need to count how many variants are exact matches, near-matches, or unexpected relative to the known mock composition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/asv-exact-partial-miss-categorization))
- **asv-feature-table-generation**: Use when raw paired-end 16S amplicon FASTQ reads (e.g., 2 x 300 bp Illumina MiSeq libraries such as V1–V2, V3–V4, V5–V7, V7–V9) must be denoised into ASVs as an alternative to OTU binning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/asv-feature-table-generation))
- **asv-inference-with-swarm**: Use when running PEMA on quality-filtered, dereplicated Illumina .fastq.gz amplicon reads (16S/18S/ITS/12S/COI) and the study objective calls for ASV inference rather than VSEARCH OTU clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/asv-inference-with-swarm))
